# REEXAMINATION: EVERYTHING AS POTENTIAL EDGE

Owner order, Sun 2026-11-15: once the master list exists, go back and reexamine every single thing on it as POTENTIAL EDGE and POTENTIAL ALPHA. Inputs of record: `MASTER-CHECKED-LIST-2026-11-13-15.md` (the master list, STATUS PARTIAL with one named gap) and its companion `MASTER-CHECKED-LIST-ADDENDUM-SIBLING.md`, which landed while this lane was working and whose items are included in full (group AA below). The Potential-Edge Census scored the top tier and counted the long tail without scoring it; this file finishes that job item by item.

## THE MEASURE

The owner's measure for this hunt, stated Sun 2026-11-15 08:58 ET and applied to every read below: **we are not looking for PROVEN TRADES; we are looking for POTENTIAL EDGE.** Proof requires a forward window and a time machine. The product of this file is candidates whose evidence survives honest deflation at their true trial count, with a mechanism named, a falsifier registered, and accrual wired or named. How success is measured matters as much as the methods, so the tiers below grade potential, not proof:

- **LIVE CANDIDATE**: armed and accruing, or registered with dated reads ahead; its falsifier exists and its first numbers have dates.
- **WATCH**: evidence suggestive, the deciding test not yet runnable or not yet run; the gap field names the single addition that most raises its grade.
- **MACHINERY**: not an edge object itself, but it raises other objects' grades (measurement, accrual, deflation, falsifier integrity).
- **RESOLVED-AGAINST**: its own pre-registered falsifier or its own resolved sample already spoke, with the pointer in the read. Nothing here is graded against on external literature; external evidence designed tests, it never delivered verdicts.
- **UNGRADABLE-BLOCKED**: a named blocker (data, access, reconciliation) stands between the object and any lawful grade.

Every item gets five fields: what it is; its evidence exactly as printed in its source (numbers, denominators, dates), with NUMBERLESS printed where the source carries no numbers; its mechanism, or MECHANISM-ABSENT; its gap; its tier. Typed seats (Decisions API, gpt-6-luna) give each item a mechanism-plausibility read (P) and a most-likely-null read (N) as a lens. Seats never set tiers; the five fields do, computed from the record. Seven-lens spot panels ran on the items where the seat read surprised (section 3).

## 2. THE SEAT LENS (bulk)

Typed seats over the whole table: 1,470 questions attempted in 34 batched calls (bank `potential-edge-reexamination` v1.0, gpt-6-luna, shared per-group evidence packs); 1,278 answers returned for 639 of the 735 items, cost $0.035358. Every seated item carries its two probabilities in its read below (P = mechanism plausibility, or for machinery the probability it materially raises other objects' grades; N = the adversarial null's probability). Two calls failed on the provider's tokens-per-minute rate limit (HTTP 429, after the harness's own retries): F. Wave 4 factory G (ML S8), Addendum (sibling corpus) / ABYDV. Those 96 items (group F, reads F1-F25; addendum reads AA130-AA200) carry 'seats: not returned' below and their tiers stand on the record alone, which is where tiers come from in any case. The 429 is a hard stop for this provider in this lane's attempt: the two packs were not retried and the spot panels of section 3 were not run, and both are named as this lane's remainder in the STATUS line for a later run to complete.

How the lens was used, per the tasking: seats are a way of looking, not a vote. Where a seat strongly disagreed with the tier the record suggests, the item went to a seven-lens spot panel (section 3) and the disagreement is preserved in print. No tier in this file was set or changed by a seat.

## 3. SURPRISES AND SPOT PANELS

Surprise rule, computed from the record: (a) tier RESOLVED-AGAINST or UNGRADABLE-BLOCKED with seat plausibility at or above 0.60 (the seat sees life where the record closed the door); (b) tier LIVE CANDIDATE with plausibility at or below 0.20, or tier WATCH (edge kind) with plausibility at or below 0.10 and null at or above 0.85 (the seat sees nothing where the record keeps a door open).

- W4 Trial row 5: Sprint 1 Candidate B (earnings cross-sectional gate): tier UNGRADABLE-BLOCKED, seat P 0.68 / N 0.61. ### Group J. Overnight/tax/weekend/candidates/queue (ML S8) (3 reads)

**J1 · Overnight Focus-names edge (census C01)** — LIVE CANDIDATE · seats P 0.33 / N 0.28
What: Holding the Focus complex close-to-open, harvested through closing/opening auctions on five carry names. Trial Register row 1.
Evidence: Discovery exam: DSR 0.99994, SPA 0.001 vs SPY / 0.002 vs QQQ, PBO 0.067, t 4.97, gross Sharpe 1.67, 2,229 sessions to 2018; at the falsifier's pre-registered family count of 10, DSR 0.99910. Lane 9 widening: basket +16.46bp/night t 4.97, halves t 3.61 / 3.48, ex-gap-flag +12.24bp t 5.24, all 29 names positive, SPY +3.53 / QQQ +4.85 same nights. Alive at 5bp friction, dead at 15bp; crossing spreads ~-27bp/night; true auction cost ~5.8bp; options form dead on real chains (weekend theta 5-15x the drift). Recent 24 nights ex-earnings: -56.31bp, t -0.79 (the cold window).
Mechanism: The overnight premium concentrated in high-beta names: risk and information accumulate while the market is closed and are paid to whoever holds through the close; the auction-only harvest is the difference source, since crossing spreads (27bp) exceeds the premium itself.
Gap: Nothing structural: the 60-session forward falsifier on auction prints starts Mon 2026-11-16 (U4); demotion if mean net of 5bp is not positive. The print basis is the one addition that mattered and it landed Sun.

### Group M. Lane 3 private data (ML S9) (7 reads)

**M1 · Near-miss portfolio (not computable, now curable)** — WATCH · seats P 0.30 / N 0.85
What: The portfolio of rejected entries that almost traded: the estate's largest private dataset, unmeasurable at Lane 3's run.
Evidence: 2,354 dispatcher rejections; the true entry near-miss class is 773 insufficient_slice_bp blocks (552 from three mirrored investandbefree_dmac variants at exactly 184 each); historical records carried no symbol/side/price; only testable reject class n=6 (LANE3).
Mechanism: Rejected entries are the gate's opinion made visible: if the gate is wrong, the near-miss portfolio outperforms the taken one, and nobody outside this estate has this data. This is Morillo T3 in its purest local form.
Gap: Landed Sun: U2 stamps symbol/side/ref_price on every rejection from Mon. The first graded near-miss shadow portfolio is the COMMITMENTS proof event completing across Monday's session.

**M2 · Conjunction scarcity + mirror-twin breadth** — WATCH · seats P 0.14 / N 0.88
What: Measurement of real cross-sleeve conjunctions and of how much fleet breadth is mechanical mirroring.
Evidence: 849 buy_to_open fills, 39 (day, symbol) groups, exactly 1 with 2+ distinct sleeves; dozens of aster sleeves carry byte-identical journal event counts (mirror twins counted twice). Lead-lag not computable at Lane 3's run (intents lacked symbols except BTC sleeves).
Mechanism: Two objects in one: (a) conjunction scarcity caps every conjunction claim (J23, J36, J39) at a base rate near zero historically; (b) mirror-twin breadth means fleet-level breadth statistics overstate independent agreement by construction.
Gap: Forward conjunction measurement on U2's stamped intents from Mon; the mirror-twin correction is a counting rule the family cards (W8) must apply.

**M3 · Fills as signal (book + dispatcher)** — WATCH · seats P 0.37 / N 0.90
What: Whether the estate's own fills predict anything.
Evidence: Book resolved sample: 4 trades, 4 losses, -$2,460.13 (SHOP, MO, ELF, NVDA). Dispatcher buy fills with bar coverage n=95, all from one imported session (1,090 of 1,125 fills are 2026-11-12). Fleet intent intensity vs next-day QQQ corr 0.046 over 15 days.
Mechanism: A fill is a decision that survived every gate; if fills carry no forward information, the gates are the product and the signals are decoration. The corr 0.046 on 15 days is a null at a useless n, printed as such.
Gap: Resolved book trades: the sample grows one trade at a time and is the slowest clock in the estate; nothing accelerates it honestly.

**M4 · Segment decomposition (Lane 3 form)** — WATCH · seats P 0.41 / N 0.74
What: Session-segment decomposition of returns on the 1Min cache. Census C03's source; Lane 9's widening is S3.
Evidence: 21 symbols x 14 sessions = 294 symbol-sessions: overnight +15.6bp mean, open drive +11.7bp, midday -28.9bp (win 43%, the only persistently negative segment), close hour -7.8bp; full intraday -24.8bp. MSFT/AMZN overnight flagged earnings-gap contaminated (LANE3).
Mechanism: Midday is where this estate's names give back the open's information move; the claim is the ordering of segments, not any level. A6 placebo sleeves (T6) run segment-forced as its null.

**M5 · Fleet intent clock + unfed share** — MACHINERY · seats P 0.79 / N 0.62
What: The fleet's intent timing distribution and the unfed share of its journal.
Evidence: Intents print in every ET hour with no RTH concentration (a machine clock); 36.0% of all 1,353,045 journal lines are unfed evaluations (LANE3).
Mechanism: A fleet that evaluates uniformly around the clock is spending its scarcest resource (host CPU, B25) on hours with no market; the unfed share prices how much of the fleet's record is non-events. As alpha: none claimed; as a cost map it feeds every efficiency grade.
Gap: Nothing; it is a diagnostic that already changed how the record is read (Lane 6's register carries the unfed share as a placebo matching statistic).

### Group Q. Lane 7 sweep (ML S9) (15 reads)

**Q1 · Anti-Midna control line (sweep form)** — RESOLVED-AGAINST · seats P 0.16 / N 0.67
What: The sweep's one graded separating object: the anti-Midna control portfolio. Fully autopsied as S1.
Evidence: Control +268.80bp (t 4.37, n=58); Midna selection excess over it -3.33bp. Lane 9 decomposition: universe term +272.13bp (t 6.64, hit 0.879), idiosyncratic term -3.34bp (t -0.08, hit 0.483); night-clustered, the anti book itself is t 0.95 on 4 nights.
Mechanism: As an edge object the inversion is resolved against (S1). As machinery the standing control line (night study step 11) is the estate's only instrument that can convict or clear the study's selection, and it accrues nightly.
Gap: Forward control-line reads: the frozen 2026-11-13 cohort reads Mon 2026-11-16, and every night study adds one more excess observation.

**Q2 · Lead: RSI=OS fwd5** — WATCH · seats P 0.55 / N 0.85
What: Panel lead: oversold RSI state at the 5-session horizon. Census C09's panel arm.
Evidence: +521bp, t 3.09, q 0.69, on 4 nights (LANE7). Does not pass the separator rule fixed before results were read.
Mechanism: Short-horizon oversold mean reversion (the K2 oversold complex, census Section 4c: one object at seat probability 0.69).
Gap: Independent nights: the panel re-runs at ~20 study nights (from 8); the family arbitration in K2 grades it at family N, not cell N.

**Q3 · Lead: RSI=OS x MACD=BEAR- fwd5** — WATCH · seats P 0.55 / N 0.91
What: Panel pair lead: oversold RSI jointly with bearish MACD state.
Evidence: +527bp, q 0.715 (LANE7).
Mechanism: The pair claims oversold-in-downtrend differs from oversold-in-uptrend: capitulation inside a falling tape as the bounce's precondition.
Gap: Same nights addition as Q2; K2's signal-overlap check decides whether it is a separate object from Q2 at all.

**Q4 · Lead: VOLR=HEAVY fwd5** — WATCH · seats P 0.42 / N 0.84
What: Panel lead: heavy volume-ratio state at fwd5. Census second-tier table.
Evidence: t 3.39, q 0.691 (LANE7).
Mechanism: Heavy volume at the read marks participation climax; the fwd5 claim is that climax volume precedes drift rather than reversal in this complex.
Gap: Same panel re-run at ~20 nights.

**Q5 · RSI=HIGH x POS10=MID fwd5 (the warning)** — MACHINERY · seats P 0.77 / N 0.94
What: The sweep's poster child for the nights floor: a huge t on almost no nights.
Evidence: t 10.01 on 3 nights (LANE7, printed as the warning, not the lead).
Mechanism: MECHANISM-ABSENT as a claim; as an exhibit it is the file's cleanest proof that position-count t values lie and night-clustered units are the honest denominator.
Gap: Nothing; it is retained as the standing warning example.

**Q6 · Forecast midday slot (census C12)** — WATCH · seats P 0.08 / N 0.87
What: The forecast corpus's strongest object: midday grading slot hit rate.
Evidence: 90.2% hit, n=61, z 2.79, BH q 0.188: strongest forecast object, still not separating under the pre-fixed rule (n >= 20, q <= 0.10). Slot cells are correlated by re-grading (LANE7).
Mechanism: No mechanism claimed in its source, and the census seat panel agreed (null probability 0.78, the highest on its board). The honest read: a forecaster-quality artefact candidate, not a market object.
Gap: Lane 8's accrual reform: grade each forecast line once at final revision over 20 grading days, which removes the re-grading correlation that currently makes the cell untestable.

**Q7 · Forecast slot drags: night_study 57.9%, pre_close 60.5%** — WATCH · seats P 0.07 / N 0.89
What: The forecast corpus's weak slots.
Evidence: Hit rates on the parsed corpus (311 of the log's 319 tallies; the 8-gap is the explained two-leg double count, master list D5).
Mechanism: A slot whose calls grade worst is a statement about the forecaster's process at that hour, not about the market; its use is process repair, and its pair (Q6) is the same instrument read from the top.
Gap: Same 20-grading-day clean re-grade as Q6.

**Q8 · Forecast confidence ordering** — WATCH · seats P 0.50 / N 0.71
What: Stated confidence orders outcomes correctly across the resolved forecast corpus.
Evidence: High 0.878 / medium 0.775 / low 0.688 hit rates (LANE7), monotone, without separating under the sweep's rule.
Mechanism: The forecaster's confidence statements carry real information about the forecaster's own accuracy: a calibration object. If it holds forward, confidence-scaled sizing (queue 13, J35) has a measured input instead of a narrative one. The census did not score this object anywhere.
Gap: Forward grading under the axis-tag accrual (R6), where confidence is a per-call field: 20 grading days gives the first clean forward read of the ordering.

**Q9 · Forecast Twin (sweep-resolved form)** — RESOLVED-AGAINST · seats P 0.16 / N 0.89
What: The typed-seat Forecast Twin's 37 poses, resolved sweep-side by Lane 7.
Evidence: Twin Brier 0.2165 vs climatology 0.1972 (skill -0.098), miscalibrated in both directions by bucket; Midna's joined calls hit 0.811; on disagreements (n=8) Midna hit 0.750 (LANE7). Falsifier clock stands at 300 (37 of 300 resolved).
Mechanism: A typed-seat forecaster graded against its own record: negative at first read. The disagreement subset (Midna 0.750 on n=8) is K3's across-agent arm in miniature.
Gap: Accrual to 300 poses under the standing resolver machinery (U12 verified the twin ledger carries all 37 resolution rows).

**Q10 · Seat-twin on withheld symbols** — MACHINERY · seats P 0.35 / N 0.72
What: The plausibility seat run as a forecaster on 90 withheld-symbol state vectors.
Evidence: Seat Brier 0.2516 vs climatology 0.2421; directional hit 51.1% vs 58.9% base rate. The seat leans momentum-continuation with RSI, which the panel does not support (LANE7).
Mechanism: MECHANISM-ABSENT as an edge object; as a measurement it prices the typed seat's standalone forecasting at worse than climatology, which is why seats in this estate flag and generate but never size.
Gap: Nothing; the measurement is the deliverable and it is already priced into every seat's standing.

**Q11 · Discretization seat flag (coarser buckets, 6 of 9 axes)** — MACHINERY · seats P 0.71 / N 0.70
What: The sweep's discretization seat defended coarser buckets for 6 of 9 axes.
Evidence: Flag only (LANE7); the seat's plausibility ratings on all 24 strongest cells were 0.01-0.04 probability real, agreeing with the deflation.
Mechanism: MECHANISM-ABSENT (a methods flag). Coarser buckets trade resolution for cell counts; on an 8-night panel that trade is usually right, which is why the flag matters for the re-run's design.
Gap: The ~20-night panel re-run adopts or rejects it in its design, in writing.

**Q12 · Panel state-cells remainder (17,790 cells)** — UNGRADABLE-BLOCKED · seats P 0.06 / N 0.97
What: All panel state-cells graded by the sweep beyond the named leads: singles, pairs and supported triples across the panel's axes and horizons.
Evidence: 17,790 panel state-cells graded; separators ZERO at every horizon, order and corpus under the pre-fixed rule (panel: n >= 30, nights >= 4, |nightly-cluster t| >= 3, BH q <= 0.10). At 8-night horizons nothing exceeds |t| 1.11. Panel: 499 observations, only 8 independent nights, fwd5 only 4 nights (194 obs). Register: GESTALT-SWEEP-REGISTER.jsonl, 24,517 rows counted back.

**R4 · A21 earnings gradient (census C05 source)** — WATCH · seats P 0.52 / N 0.74
What: Expected-move gradient in the earnings archive: exceed rate falls as the priced move grows.
Evidence: 182 events: realised exceeded priced in 18.7% overall; by tercile em_low 12/62 = 0.194, em_mid 15/60 = 0.250, em_high 7/60 = 0.117; coverage ratio mean 0.68 (LANE8 retrospective rows).
Mechanism: Implied earnings moves overstate most exactly where they are largest: the options market prices the tail it fears, and the fear is size-dependent. This gradient conditions the whole earnings-vol family (E3, J26, J41, O34).
Gap: Forward archive accrual per earnings print; F5's falsifier reads by tercile from the start (census K4 check i).

**R5 · A21 x A22 forecast interaction (band | macro)** — WATCH · seats P 0.50 / N 0.82
What: Forecast-side interaction: expected-move band conditioned on macro-event state, on graded retrospective forecast rows.
Evidence: band|macro 8/9 = 0.889 vs band|none 10/26 = 0.385 (LANE8); the pair A21xA22 is graded NOW at n=201 forecast rows (plus n=182 archive for the marginal). Lane 8 registered the interaction cell as a forward hypothesis, not a result: the key cell is n=9.
Mechanism: A band call is a range claim; on macro-event days the range is set by the event, so a band call that agrees with the event's own pricing inherits the event's information, while the same band on a none day is the forecaster's unaided range sense. The interaction, if real, says the forecaster's range skill is event-borrowed, and sizing band calls by event state is the tradable form.
Gap: Forward accrual under the axis-tag schema (R6): A21 and A22 are both tagged axes, so the interaction accrues at forecast speed from Mon; the gate is the interim standard (cells at n >= 10 with Wilson intervals, then the pair's own falsifier at the Lane 8 table's rates).

Accrual starts Mon 2026-11-16 at ~5 graded calls/night; the other four sessions are a named follow-on (LANE8).
Mechanism: MECHANISM-ABSENT (the wiring is the fastest gradability machine in the estate: it converts every future forecast into 11-axis evidence at zero marginal research cost).
Gap: Extending the tags to the other four daily sessions multiplies accrual ~4x (Lane 8's own rate table: mid-December vs February reads for R12/R13 turn on exactly this).

**R7 · Hierarchical pooling rule (tested, NOT adopted)** — RESOLVED-AGAINST · seats P 0.18 / N 0.83
What: Lane 8's pre-registered pooling rule (k=16 shrinkage toward family mean), tested on split-half Brier/log-loss and rejected by its own falsifier.
Evidence: Loses on A05xA06 (0.2275/0.6402 vs 0.2095/0.5902 unpooled) and on A12xA15; wins big only on sparse A34xA03; seed stability 2/5, 2/5, 4/5 (LANE8). Interim standard adopted instead: raw rate + n + Wilson interval; cells under n=10 report UNGRADABLE-YET with a date.
Mechanism: A shrinkage rule that cannot beat the raw rate on its own test data is not a conservative estimator, it is a worse one; the estate correctly refused it. The per-family empirical-Bayes variant is registered as a new hypothesis for forward data only.
Gap: Forward data under R6 is the variant's only legitimate test bed.

### Group S. Lane 9 deeper (ML S9) (4 reads)

**S1 · Inversion autopsy (anti-Midna decomposition)** — RESOLVED-AGAINST · seats P 0.05 / N 0.96
What: Exact decomposition of the anti-Midna control's return into universe and selection terms, replicated to the basis point first.
Evidence: Anti +268.80bp = universe term +272.13bp (t 6.64, hit 0.879) + idiosyncratic term -3.34bp (t -0.08, hit 0.483), n=58 positions over 4 nights. 101% of the pooled return is the universe term. Concentration: TARGET 2026-11-11 is 70.4% of the pooled net; CRWV 19.5% of gross |P&L|. Night-clustered anti book: t 0.95 on 4 nights. Spec discrepancy printed: the spec's prose defines an always-short control (+170.34bp pooled, excess +98.45bp a pure sign-mismatch artefact); the founding table computes the sign-matched control (LANE9 S1).
Mechanism: The verdict in the file's own terms: the control's return is universe beta in the strict sense (the complex's own overnight return, sign-flipped), the inverted selection adds nothing, and the study's per-name conclusions were one-signed per night, so there was no per-name information to invert. What survives is K1's premium and the standing control line as the selection-excess meter.
Gap: The forward cohort frozen at the 2026-11-13 close (29 names, 28 short bias) reads overnight Mon 2026-11-16 under the control line; the excess term is the only number that can ever convict or clear the study's selection.

**S2 · Segment widening: overnight leg** — LIVE CANDIDATE · seats P 0.54 / N 0.36
What: Lane 3's overnight segment recomputed on the full Focus complex over the longest stored history.
Evidence: Basket +16.46bp over 2,229 nights, t 4.97 (night-level, already clustered); halves +14.88 (t 3.61) and +18.03 (t 3.48); ex-gap-flag (10.69% of name-nights removed) +12.24bp at t 5.24; all 29 names positive (SNDK +44.10, CRWV +41.08, RKLB +31.98 top; XLE +5.33 bottom); SPY +3.53, QQQ +4.85 same nights. Caveats on the face: daily-bar open basis, not auction prints; last five weeks cold (-56.31bp ex-earnings, t -0.79, 24 nights) (LANE9 S2).
Mechanism: This is J1 measured a second way on a second sample definition and surviving: the widening is the census's E3 evidence for C01, not a new object.
Gap: The print-basis falsifier (U4) is the deciding instrument; the widening retires the 'small sample' objection permanently.

**S3 · Segment widening: midday leg** — WATCH · seats P 0.33 / N 0.77
What: Lane 3's midday segment recomputed on the full 21-symbol cache and the Focus-6 subset.
Evidence: Direction replicates: all-21 pooled -33.69bp (Lane 3: -28.9), Focus-6 pooled -81.63bp with all six names negative (NVDA win 0.077). Session-clustered t fails: -1.53 all-21, -1.82 Focus-6, 13 sessions. Sub-period unstable on Focus-6 (first half -154.24bp win 0.000; second half -19.40bp). Verdict in the file: a live hypothesis with the right sign, not a survivor (LANE9 S2).
Mechanism: As M4: midday give-back of the open's information move. The widening's contribution is negative knowledge: the effect halves across the window, which a pooled read would never have shown.
Gap: Cached sessions: the 1Min cache accrues one session per day; at ~20+ sessions the clustered test gets its first honest power.

**S4 · STACK-CELL-JOINT-BELOW-20261115 (registered candidate)** — LIVE CANDIDATE · seats P 0.38 / N 0.91
What: Lane 9's registered forward candidate: the (mixed_below, mixed_below) stack-posture cell as a forecast-quality marker (a conviction haircut candidate for cards whose underlying sits in the cell; not a trade signal, not a sizing input until graded).
Evidence: Retrospective cell: 3/11 = 0.273, date-adjusted -0.456 vs its own dates' other calls; composition-confounded (NVDA/RKLB/ALAB bullet calls 2026-11-12/13 vs the AGREE-BULL cell's MO/EW/EXPD defensives 2026-11-04/06); cells barely coexist in time (LANE9 S3). Grading gate: >= 150 forward joint rows AND >= 25 JOINT-BELOW rows AND >= 20 nights AND both cells populated on 6+ distinct nights (~25 nights all-session, 65+ night-study-only). Falsifier: dead if forward hit(JOINT-BELOW) >= hit(all others) - 5pp, or the AGREE-BULL gap is under +10pp; UNGRADABLE-YET if coexistence fails at 40 nights.
Mechanism: Joint posture below both stacks in mixed order marks reads built on a trend structure that is neither trending nor based; the date-adjusted -0.456 is the one fact that survives the confound audit, and it is exactly the kind of fact a registered forward test exists to price.
Gap: Session breadth of the tag wiring (R6): all-session tagging grades it by mid-December; night-study-only pushes it to February.

Evidence: DSR at local / lab 487 / lab 991 / pooled 347: RND-7 0.922 / 0.022 / 0.007 / 0.037; REG-8 0.919 / 0.074 / 0.034 / 0.103; MTH-4 0.879 / 0.029 / 0.011 / 0.044. Each lives only at its local count of 7 or 8 and is labeled exactly that. Auction-cost interaction costs 0.85bp of mean and changes no verdict. Mean placement discriminates where Sharpe placement cannot: RND-7 (+20.48bp) beats 50 of 50 PERM and 42 of 50 LAG replicates; REG-8 (+15.27bp) beats 50 of 50 PERM but 0 of 50 LAG; MTH-4 (+14.24bp) splits LAG 27 of 50.
Mechanism: The conditioners' local DSRs are count artifacts, and the record says so; the live residue is narrower and real: RND-7's mean beats both null families, which is a conditioner-grade fact (it raises a host signal's mean) rather than a standalone edge, and the mean-placement method is now the lab's rule for gated series.
Gap: A host signal for RND-7 to condition forward: the conditioner has no standalone trade, so its grade rises only when paired and accrued, which Batch 4's conditioner class (AA64 cell 8) is the named venue for.

Mechanism: MECHANISM-ABSENT (a methods correction that weakens a verdict's margin without reversing it; printed as such).

**AA57 · ADD-W7-23 GAP-HEDGE-1 fails as specified** — RESOLVED-AGAINST · seats P 0.14 / N 0.76
What: One frozen specification: the basket's overnight leg hedged by SPY's overnight gap at the trailing 252-session beta, prior sessions only.
Evidence: n 1,977, net +6.55bp, Sharpe 1.18, t 3.31, DSR 0.000 at lab 991, against the unhedged basket's +11.46bp at Sharpe 1.163 on the same nights. The hedge removes mean and variance in the same proportion.
Mechanism: The denominator attack (keep the drift, cut the variance with the index gap) fails in this form, measured: the Focus premium is not separable from the index gap by a trailing beta, which is itself anatomy the A-2 world read (AA84) predicts.
Gap: Nothing for this form; gap-hedge variants are not re-run without a new mechanism (Wave 8 cell 8's own bar).

**AA119 · ADD-FDY-55 C-2 residue: attention is not the price** — WATCH · seats P 0.30 / N 0.84

**AA143 · ADD-ABY-14 A-2 world split: EWJ** — WATCH · seats not returned
What: The ON_focus29 basket's 2,229 nights split by EWJ's own overnight leg sign and tercile (the tracker prices the foreign session that runs inside the US night).
Evidence: EWJ: world-up nights +83.4bp (n 1,119), world-down -51.0bp (n 1,110), diff +134.4bp, Welch t 22.4; tercile diff +186.1bp, t 23.7. Unconditional basket mean over the same nights +16.5bp.
Mechanism: Anatomy of the find, stated honestly in the source: the tracker's gap and the Focus gaps are two expressions of one global overnight move, so the split is contemporaneous, not leading, and cannot gate the registered leg. What it establishes is that the premium is the US expression of a global overnight drift, present on world-up nights and negative on world-down nights, which kills every US-idiosyncratic story about the find.
Gap: An honest ES/NQ night-session series (Wave 8 cell W8-2): the drift's futures-market expression is the tradeable form of this anatomy, and no conditioner claim is lawful before that series exists.

What it establishes is that the premium is the US expression of a global overnight drift, present on world-up nights and negative on world-down nights, which kills every US-idiosyncratic story about the find.
Gap: An honest ES/NQ night-session series (Wave 8 cell W8-2): the drift's futures-market expression is the tradeable form of this anatomy, and no conditioner claim is lawful before that series exists.

**AA151 · ADD-ABY-22 A-3 BTC weekend conditioner** — WATCH · seats not returned

**AA181 · ADD-DV-06 Dive T6: Hawkes branching ratio** — WATCH · seats not returned
What: Territory 6 (self-found): the point-process endogeneity of the print stream, the Hawkes branching ratio as a tape statistic.
Evidence: Claims 6.1 to 6.3 graded in the file (branching ratio between zero and one, reported 0.6 to 0.7 for index futures and IBM/AAPL in the literature; the near-criticality reading fragile to event definition, Claim 6.2 HIGH). Cheapest honest probe printed: an event as a mid price change persisting at least one second, per name per session the share of events followed by another within one second above the time-shuffled null, asking only whether that proxy on day t predicts the absolute size, not the sign, of that night's overnight move; three choices charged as the trial count. Breaking point: the proxy collapses when the persistence cut moves from one second to two.
Mechanism: Endogeneity as a conditioner is the one tape statistic A-1's mirage argues FOR rather than against: the tape's self-excitation is a regime variable, and regime variables are what the find's forward window needs. It feeds Wave 8 cell W8-6 with the event definition frozen in advance.
Gap: The probe itself, runnable on the held A-1 corpus tonight: the corpus exists (AA136), the definition is frozen in the transfer, and the outcome (absolute overnight size) is the one target the weekend's work never aimed a tape statistic at.

**AA182 · ADD-DV-07 Dive T7: mempool congestion** — WATCH · seats not returned
What: Territory 7 (self-found): the Bitcoin settlement queue as a non-price ledger, mempool congestion as an exogenous demand gauge.
Evidence: Claims 7.1 to 7.3 graded in the file (congestion is the main determinant of confirmation delay in a structural fee-market model; fee estimators work for one to six block horizons; no credible source found claims mempool congestion predicts equity or crypto returns, recorded as a directed negative finding at LOW). Cheapest honest probe printed: one daily number from mempool.space (median fee rate for next-block confirmation at the US equity close) against the next overnight return of the three most crypto-exposed names in the complex, named before the test, against matched low-congestion days; breaking point: the fee variable adds nothing once the same-day BTC absolute return is controlled for.
Mechanism: A non-price demand gauge is the rarest input class in this corpus: every other conditioner is derived from prices. The dive's own negative finding caps the prior, which is why the probe is one variable, one name set, one outcome.
Gap: The probe itself: the data route is free and daily, the name set is named in the transfer's terms (the complex's three most crypto-exposed names, fixed before the test), and the control is printed.

## 6. THE STRONGEST ITEM THE CENSUS MISSED

**R5, the A21 x A22 forecast interaction** (Lane 8's retrospective corpus, read in group R above). The census scored the A21 marginal gradient (its C05, on the 182-event earnings archive) and never touched the forecast-log interaction, although it is graded on more rows and points the other way: conditioning is where the signal lives.

Evidence, exactly as printed in Lane 8's source: on the 201 graded forecast-log rows (base hit rate 0.657), band calls (the A21 expected-move band axis) hit **8/9 = 0.889 when a macro event was present (A22)** against **10/26 = 0.385 when none was**. The mechanism is named and narrow: a band call is a claim about a priced move; on macro days the event itself supplies the move's information, so the band call inherits the event's direction, while on quiet days the same call is a coin flip against drift. The gap is equally concrete: forward rows at the interaction's own denominator, accruing from Mon 2026-11-16 through the step-13 axis tags (R6), with the symmetric pairs A22 x A23 and A15 x A23 already registered as accrual-only companions (R12, R13; first reads Dec 2026 to Feb 2027). Tier WATCH, not LIVE CANDIDATE, for one honest reason: its retrospective cells are small (9 and 26) and composition-checked only in part, so the deciding test is the forward accrual, which is wired but has not printed a row yet. It is the strongest missed item because it is the only unscored object in the corpus that is simultaneously graded now, mechanistically named, and already accruing. Runner-up: Q8, the forecast confidence ordering (0.878 / 0.775 / 0.688 across 311 parsed calls), a calibration object the census likewise never scored.

## 8. ADDENDUM — REMAINDER RUN (Sun 2026-11-15)

This addendum completes the two remainders named in the original STATUS line. The v1.0 lane treated the provider's HTTP 429 as a hard stop; that reading was wrong. The harness's standing doctrine (decisions_harness.py header) is that a 429 is backed off and resumed, never a stop order, and the only local limit is the 5,000 requests/day circuit breaker (97 requests stood in the day ledger when this run finished). Both remainder runs therefore went out under bank `potential-edge-reexamination` v1.1, continuing v1.0's versioning, with a long-backoff resume wrapper around the harness call. For the two seat packs, the rebuilt pack text's sha256 was verified against the pack_hash recorded for the failed v1.0 calls before any request was sent (F group 58edc7f0..., ABYDV 4f38e564...): same items, same pack, same questions; only the outcome differs. Neither call needed a resume this time; both answered in full on the first attempt. Seats and panels are lens only, here as everywhere in this file: no tier was set or changed by anything in this section, and the reads in section 4 still print 'seats not returned' for these 96 items because the body is the historical record of the v1.0 attempt; the returned values are printed here, beside the record-computed tiers.

### 8.2 Re-screen of the 96 under section 3's rules

Section 3's screening covered only the 639 items seated in v1.0. The 96 remainder items were screened on their return, under the same rules: (a) tier RESOLVED-AGAINST or UNGRADABLE-BLOCKED with plausibility at or above 0.60; (b) tier LIVE CANDIDATE with plausibility at or below 0.20, or tier WATCH (edge kind) with plausibility at or below 0.10 and null at or above 0.85. Two items fired, one under each rule, and both went to a seven-lens spot panel in this same run (section 8.3):

- Rule (a): AA193 ADD-POP-ABYSS Abyss phrase tests, counted class: tier RESOLVED-AGAINST, seat P 0.70 / N 0.55. The record's close stands; the seat's disagreement is preserved in its panel vector below.
- Rule (b): F13 G8 (registered, ID-only): tier WATCH (edge kind), seat P 0.00 / N 0.94. The record's open door stands; the seat's skepticism is preserved in its panel vector below.

No other remainder item fired either rule. The full screening population is now the whole table: 735 items screened, 28 surprises in total (26 from v1.0, printed in section 3; 2 from the remainder, printed here).

### 8.3 The seven-lens spot panels

Panels ran for the 26 items screened in section 3 (182 questions, 182 answers, $0.004418) and for the 2 remainder fires above (14 questions, 14 answers, $0.000327), under bank `potential-edge-reexamination` v1.1. Pack construction and lens wording are exactly those of the prepared runner `reexam_panels.py`; the run went out under the reexamination bank per the remainder instruction (the prepared script named its own panels bank and had never run), executed by `reexam_panels_run.py`. Each vector below is the seven lenses' mechanism-plausibility probabilities in the fixed order [HFM, SWING, GAME, GAMBLER, PREDMKT, DATA, QUANT], printed in full so the disagreement is the record, not a summary of it. The bulk seat's P and N from the item's own read are printed beside the vector for contrast. Panels preserve disagreement; they do not adjudicate it, and no tier moved.
